**152 条记录 · 13 个词群**

**本章主线：** 识别物品 → 清洁和居家 → 工具与部件 → 材料加工 → 品质评价 → 回收再用

**最后尝试回答：** 选一件家居用品，介绍它的材料、部件、用途、质量及报废去向。

### 词群 10.01｜家里有哪些物品与材料 {#group-10-01}

**串联方式：** 从物件、商品、纪念品和工艺品进入材料、必需品、装备、工具包及器具；tap 是家中水龙头。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">tap<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-001" aria-label="定位 tap">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-001" data-mark-label="tap" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tap：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tap：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tap" title="在有道查询 tap">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.轻拍，轻叩；开发</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Tap</strong></a> each item gently before packing your stuff to check for loose parts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收拾东西时，轻敲每件物品，检查有无松动部件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Among all this stuff, the fragile item will crack if you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tap</strong></a> it too hard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这堆东西中，那件易碎物品敲重了就会裂。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The repairer gave each item a light <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tap</strong></a> while sorting through the broken stuff.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修理工整理坏东西时，轻敲了每件物品。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">tap gently</span> · 轻轻敲</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=belongings&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">belongings</a></span> · 个人物品</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inspection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inspection</a></span> · 检查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-004">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-004" class="vocabulary-word-title">merchandise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-004" aria-label="定位 merchandise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-004" data-mark-label="merchandise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="merchandise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="merchandise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=merchandise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 merchandise" title="在有道查询 merchandise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-004</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 商品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum shop sells <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=merchandise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>merchandise</strong></a> inspired by an ancient artefact, including a small souvenir replica.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆商店售卖以古代人工制品为灵感的商品，包括小型纪念复制品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not mistake a souvenir from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=merchandise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>merchandise</strong></a> stall for a genuine archaeological artefact.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要把商品摊上的纪念品误当成真正的考古文物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A photograph of the artefact appears on souvenir <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=merchandise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>merchandise</strong></a> sold near the exhibition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览附近出售的纪念商品印有这件文物的照片。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">display merchandise</span> · 陈列商品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=replica&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">replica</a></span> · 复制品</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">retail</a></span> · 零售</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-005">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-005" class="vocabulary-word-title">souvenir<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-005" aria-label="定位 souvenir">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-005" data-mark-label="souvenir" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="souvenir：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="souvenir：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=souvenir&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 souvenir" title="在有道查询 souvenir">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-005</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 纪念品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum shop sells merchandise inspired by an ancient artefact, including a small <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=souvenir&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>souvenir</strong></a> replica.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆商店售卖以古代人工制品为灵感的商品，包括小型纪念复制品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not mistake a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=souvenir&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>souvenir</strong></a> from the merchandise stall for a genuine archaeological artefact.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要把商品摊上的纪念品误当成真正的考古文物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A photograph of the artefact appears on <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=souvenir&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>souvenir</strong></a> merchandise sold near the exhibition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览附近出售的纪念商品印有这件文物的照片。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">buy a souvenir</span> · 购买纪念品</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-007">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-007" class="vocabulary-word-title">material<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-007" aria-label="定位 material">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-007" data-mark-label="material" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="material：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="material：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=material&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 material" title="在有道查询 material">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-007</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 材料，原料；人才；adj. 物质的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The factory uses crude oil as a raw <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=material&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>material</strong></a> for plastic production.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工厂把原油用作塑料生产的原材料。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report compares crude oil with other raw <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=material&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>material</strong></a> sources.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告把原油与其他原材料来源作比较。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Transporting raw <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=material&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>material</strong></a> such as crude oil requires specialist equipment.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">运输原油等原材料需要专门设备。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-010">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-010" class="vocabulary-word-title">necessity<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-010" aria-label="定位 necessity">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-010" data-mark-label="necessity" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="necessity：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="necessity：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=necessity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 necessity" title="在有道查询 necessity">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-010</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 必需品，必不可少的事物；必要</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A cooking utensil is a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=necessity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>necessity</strong></a> in any camping outfit, so pack one with your repair kit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">炊具是任何露营装备的必需品，因此把一件炊具与修理工具包一起装好。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The emergency kit contains every <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=necessity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>necessity</strong></a>, from a warm outfit to a simple eating utensil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">应急包包含各种必需品，从保暖衣物到简单餐具。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before buying a full camping outfit, decide which kit is a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=necessity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>necessity</strong></a> and which utensil you can borrow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">购买全套露营装备前，先决定哪些工具是必需的、哪些用具可以借。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a basic necessity</span> · 基本必需品</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equipment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">equipment</a></span> · 装备</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=supplies&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">supplies</a></span> · 补给品</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-011">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A cooking utensil is a necessity in any camping <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=outfit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>outfit</strong></a>, so pack one with your repair kit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">炊具是任何露营装备的必需品，因此把一件炊具与修理工具包一起装好。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The emergency kit contains every necessity, from a warm <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=outfit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>outfit</strong></a> to a simple eating utensil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">应急包包含各种必需品，从保暖衣物到简单餐具。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before buying a full camping <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=outfit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>outfit</strong></a>, decide which kit is a necessity and which utensil you can borrow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">购买全套露营装备前，先决定哪些工具是必需的、哪些用具可以借。</p></li>

### 词群 10.02｜物品用完以后去哪里 {#group-10-02}

**串联方式：** 把垃圾、乱丢、废物和废品分清，再比较回收、重复使用、填埋与污水系统。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-014">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-014" class="vocabulary-word-title">garbage<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-014" aria-label="定位 garbage">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-014" data-mark-label="garbage" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="garbage：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="garbage：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=garbage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 garbage" title="在有道查询 garbage">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-014</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. [ 尤美 ] 垃圾，废物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sign says <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=garbage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>garbage</strong></a> collection, while the British leaflet says rubbish and the American one says trash.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">标牌写着 garbage collection，英国传单用 rubbish，美国传单用 trash。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Whether you call it <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=garbage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>garbage</strong></a>, rubbish or trash, put it in the correct bin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无论把垃圾叫作 garbage、rubbish 还是 trash，都要放进正确的垃圾桶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council’s multilingual guide explains where <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=garbage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>garbage</strong></a>, rubbish and trash should go.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政多语言指南说明各种垃圾该放在哪里。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">collect garbage</span> · 收垃圾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">refuse</a></span> · 废弃物</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bin</a></span> · 垃圾桶</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-015">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-015" class="vocabulary-word-title">rubbish<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-015" aria-label="定位 rubbish">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-015" data-mark-label="rubbish" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rubbish：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rubbish：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rubbish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rubbish" title="在有道查询 rubbish">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-015</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. [ 尤英 ] 垃圾，废弃物，废话</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sign says garbage collection, while the British leaflet says <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rubbish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rubbish</strong></a> and the American one says trash.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">标牌写着 garbage collection，英国传单用 rubbish，美国传单用 trash。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Whether you call it garbage, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rubbish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rubbish</strong></a> or trash, put it in the correct bin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无论把垃圾叫作 garbage、rubbish 还是 trash，都要放进正确的垃圾桶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council’s multilingual guide explains where garbage, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rubbish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rubbish</strong></a> and trash should go.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政多语言指南说明各种垃圾该放在哪里。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a rubbish bin</span> · 垃圾桶</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">refuse</a></span> · 废弃物</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bin</a></span> · 垃圾桶</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-016">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-016" class="vocabulary-word-title">trash<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-016" aria-label="定位 trash">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-016" data-mark-label="trash" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="trash：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="trash：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 trash" title="在有道查询 trash">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-016</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. [ 美 ] 垃圾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sign says garbage collection, while the British leaflet says rubbish and the American one says <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trash</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">标牌写着 garbage collection，英国传单用 rubbish，美国传单用 trash。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Whether you call it garbage, rubbish or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trash</strong></a>, put it in the correct bin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无论把垃圾叫作 garbage、rubbish 还是 trash，都要放进正确的垃圾桶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council’s multilingual guide explains where garbage, rubbish and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trash</strong></a> should go.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政多语言指南说明各种垃圾该放在哪里。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">take out the trash</span> · 倒垃圾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">refuse</a></span> · 废弃物</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bin</a></span> · 垃圾桶</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-017">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-017" class="vocabulary-word-title">recycle<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-017" aria-label="定位 recycle">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-017" data-mark-label="recycle" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="recycle：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="recycle：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recycle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 recycle" title="在有道查询 recycle">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-017</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 使再循环，回收利用；反复应用</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Reuse the bag, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recycle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recycle</strong></a> the bottle and put any litter in a bin to reduce waste.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">重复使用袋子、回收瓶子，并把垃圾放进垃圾桶，以减少废弃物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Schools can reduce waste when students reuse containers, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recycle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recycle</strong></a> paper and stop dropping litter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生重复使用容器、回收纸张并不再乱扔垃圾时，学校就能减少废弃物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Instead of leaving litter, take your waste home, reuse what you can and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recycle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recycle</strong></a> the rest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要留下垃圾，应把废弃物带回家，尽量重复使用，其他的回收处理。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">recycle glass</span> · 回收玻璃</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sorting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sorting</a></span> · 分类</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=circular%20economy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">circular economy</a></span> · 循环经济</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-018">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-018" class="vocabulary-word-title">reuse<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-018" aria-label="定位 reuse">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-018" data-mark-label="reuse" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reuse：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reuse：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reuse" title="在有道查询 reuse">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-018</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 再次使用</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Reuse</strong></a> the bag, recycle the bottle and put any litter in a bin to reduce waste.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">重复使用袋子、回收瓶子，并把垃圾放进垃圾桶，以减少废弃物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Schools can reduce waste when students <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reuse</strong></a> containers, recycle paper and stop dropping litter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生重复使用容器、回收纸张并不再乱扔垃圾时，学校就能减少废弃物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Instead of leaving litter, take your waste home, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reuse</strong></a> what you can and recycle the rest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要留下垃圾，应把废弃物带回家，尽量重复使用，其他的回收处理。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reuse a bag</span> · 重复使用袋子</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sorting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sorting</a></span> · 分类</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=circular%20economy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">circular economy</a></span> · 循环经济</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-019">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-019" class="vocabulary-word-title">litter<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-019" aria-label="定位 litter">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-019" data-mark-label="litter" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="litter：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="litter：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=litter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 litter" title="在有道查询 litter">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-019</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 垃圾，废弃物；v. 乱丢东西</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Reuse the bag, recycle the bottle and put any <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=litter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>litter</strong></a> in a bin to reduce waste.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">重复使用袋子、回收瓶子，并把垃圾放进垃圾桶，以减少废弃物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Schools can reduce waste when students reuse containers, recycle paper and stop dropping <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=litter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>litter</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生重复使用容器、回收纸张并不再乱扔垃圾时，学校就能减少废弃物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Instead of leaving <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=litter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>litter</strong></a>, take your waste home, reuse what you can and recycle the rest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要留下垃圾，应把废弃物带回家，尽量重复使用，其他的回收处理。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">drop litter</span> · 乱扔垃圾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sorting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sorting</a></span> · 分类</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=circular%20economy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">circular economy</a></span> · 循环经济</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-020">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-020" class="vocabulary-word-title">waste<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-020" aria-label="定位 waste">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-020" data-mark-label="waste" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="waste：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="waste：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=waste&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 waste" title="在有道查询 waste">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-020</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 废物；浪费；v. 浪费</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Reuse the bag, recycle the bottle and put any litter in a bin to reduce <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=waste&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>waste</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">重复使用袋子、回收瓶子，并把垃圾放进垃圾桶，以减少废弃物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Schools can reduce <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=waste&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>waste</strong></a> when students reuse containers, recycle paper and stop dropping litter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生重复使用容器、回收纸张并不再乱扔垃圾时，学校就能减少废弃物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Instead of leaving litter, take your <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=waste&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>waste</strong></a> home, reuse what you can and recycle the rest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要留下垃圾，应把废弃物带回家，尽量重复使用，其他的回收处理。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reduce waste</span> · 减少废弃物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sorting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sorting</a></span> · 分类</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=circular%20economy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">circular economy</a></span> · 循环经济</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-021">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-021" class="vocabulary-word-title">junk<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-021" aria-label="定位 junk">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-021" data-mark-label="junk" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="junk：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="junk：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=junk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 junk" title="在有道查询 junk">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-021</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 无用的东西，废物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Dumping <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=junk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>junk</strong></a> near a landfill can block the town’s sewerage system.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在垃圾填埋场附近倾倒废物会堵塞城镇排污系统。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council improved sewerage and removed <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=junk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>junk</strong></a> from an abandoned landfill.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政改善了排污系统，并清除了废弃填埋场的废物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Funding covers the sewerage network, landfill management and the collection of bulky <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=junk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>junk</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">资金用于排污管网、填埋场管理和大件废物收集。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">clear out junk</span> · 清理废物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sanitation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sanitation</a></span> · 环境卫生</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wastewater&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wastewater</a></span> · 污水</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-022">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-022" class="vocabulary-word-title">landfill<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-022" aria-label="定位 landfill">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-022" data-mark-label="landfill" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="landfill：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="landfill：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landfill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 landfill" title="在有道查询 landfill">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-022</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 垃圾填埋地；垃圾填埋</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Dumping junk near a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landfill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>landfill</strong></a> can block the town’s sewerage system.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在垃圾填埋场附近倾倒废物会堵塞城镇排污系统。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council improved sewerage and removed junk from an abandoned <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landfill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>landfill</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政改善了排污系统，并清除了废弃填埋场的废物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Funding covers the sewerage network, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landfill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>landfill</strong></a> management and the collection of bulky junk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">资金用于排污管网、填埋场管理和大件废物收集。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a landfill site</span> · 垃圾填埋场</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sanitation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sanitation</a></span> · 环境卫生</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wastewater&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wastewater</a></span> · 污水</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-023">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-023" class="vocabulary-word-title">sewerage<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-023" aria-label="定位 sewerage">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-023" data-mark-label="sewerage" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sewerage：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sewerage：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sewerage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sewerage" title="在有道查询 sewerage">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-023</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 排水系统，污水处理系统；(= sewage)污水</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Dumping junk near a landfill can block the town’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sewerage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sewerage</strong></a> system.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在垃圾填埋场附近倾倒废物会堵塞城镇排污系统。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council improved <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sewerage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sewerage</strong></a> and removed junk from an abandoned landfill.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政改善了排污系统，并清除了废弃填埋场的废物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Funding covers the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sewerage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sewerage</strong></a> network, landfill management and the collection of bulky junk.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">资金用于排污管网、填埋场管理和大件废物收集。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a sewerage system</span> · 排污系统</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sanitation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sanitation</a></span> · 环境卫生</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wastewater&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wastewater</a></span> · 污水</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 10.03｜清洁与洗浴 {#group-10-03}

**串联方式：** 先备清洁用品、浴缸和管道，再用拖把、扫帚、海绵和毛巾打扫擦洗。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-024">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-024" class="vocabulary-word-title">detergent<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-024" aria-label="定位 detergent">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-024" data-mark-label="detergent" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="detergent：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="detergent：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detergent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 detergent" title="在有道查询 detergent">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-024</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 洗涤剂</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Keep <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detergent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>detergent</strong></a> away from the shelf holding lotion, shampoo and soap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要把洗衣液放在装有润肤乳、洗发水和肥皂的架子上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The refill shop sells <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detergent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>detergent</strong></a> and shampoo, plus lotion and liquid soap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家补充装商店售卖洗衣液、洗发水、润肤乳和液体肥皂。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Guests receive shampoo, soap and lotion, while staff use <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detergent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>detergent</strong></a> for the laundry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">客人可获得洗发水、肥皂和润肤乳，员工则用洗衣液清洗衣物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">laundry detergent</span> · 洗衣液</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=toiletries&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">toiletries</a></span> · 洗漱用品</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">refill</a></span> · 补充装</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-025">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-025" class="vocabulary-word-title">lotion<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-025" aria-label="定位 lotion">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-025" data-mark-label="lotion" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="lotion：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="lotion：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lotion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 lotion" title="在有道查询 lotion">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-025</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 洁肤乳；润肤乳</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Keep detergent away from the shelf holding <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lotion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lotion</strong></a>, shampoo and soap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要把洗衣液放在装有润肤乳、洗发水和肥皂的架子上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The refill shop sells detergent and shampoo, plus <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lotion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lotion</strong></a> and liquid soap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家补充装商店售卖洗衣液、洗发水、润肤乳和液体肥皂。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Guests receive shampoo, soap and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lotion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lotion</strong></a>, while staff use detergent for the laundry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">客人可获得洗发水、肥皂和润肤乳，员工则用洗衣液清洗衣物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">body lotion</span> · 润肤乳</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=toiletries&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">toiletries</a></span> · 洗漱用品</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">refill</a></span> · 补充装</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-026">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 用洗发剂洗(头发)；n. 洗发剂</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Keep detergent away from the shelf holding lotion, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shampoo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shampoo</strong></a> and soap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要把洗衣液放在装有润肤乳、洗发水和肥皂的架子上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The refill shop sells detergent and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shampoo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shampoo</strong></a>, plus lotion and liquid soap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家补充装商店售卖洗衣液、洗发水、润肤乳和液体肥皂。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Guests receive <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shampoo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shampoo</strong></a>, soap and lotion, while staff use detergent for the laundry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">客人可获得洗发水、肥皂和润肤乳，员工则用洗衣液清洗衣物。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a bottle of shampoo</span> · 一瓶洗发水</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-028">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-028" class="vocabulary-word-title">tub<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-028" aria-label="定位 tub">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-028" data-mark-label="tub" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tub：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tub：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tub&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tub" title="在有道查询 tub">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-028</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 桶，盆；[ 美 ] 浴缸，浴盆</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A toothpaste tube fell into the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tub&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tub</strong></a> and caught near the plug above the drain pipe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一管牙膏掉进浴缸，卡在排水管上方的塞子附近。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Remove the plug from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tub&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tub</strong></a> and check the pipe before opening the sealant tube.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">打开密封胶管前，先拔掉浴缸塞并检查管道。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plumber used a flexible tube to clear the pipe after removing the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tub&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tub</strong></a> plug.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">水管工拔掉浴缸塞后，用一根软管疏通排水管。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a bath tub</span> · 浴缸</p>

</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-029">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-029" class="vocabulary-word-title">plug<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-029" aria-label="定位 plug">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-029" data-mark-label="plug" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="plug：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="plug：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plug&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 plug" title="在有道查询 plug">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-029</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 塞子；插头；v. 把……塞住</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A toothpaste tube fell into the tub and caught near the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plug&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plug</strong></a> above the drain pipe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一管牙膏掉进浴缸，卡在排水管上方的塞子附近。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Remove the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plug&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plug</strong></a> from the tub and check the pipe before opening the sealant tube.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">打开密封胶管前，先拔掉浴缸塞并检查管道。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The plumber used a flexible tube to clear the pipe after removing the tub <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plug&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plug</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">水管工拔掉浴缸塞后，用一根软管疏通排水管。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a bath plug</span> · 浴缸塞</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">drain</a></span> · 排水口</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plumbing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">plumbing</a></span> · 管道工程</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-032">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-032" class="vocabulary-word-title">mop<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-032" aria-label="定位 mop">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-032" data-mark-label="mop" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mop：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mop：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mop" title="在有道查询 mop">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-032</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 拖把；v. 用拖把擦</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Sweep with the broom, wash with the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mop</strong></a>, wipe marks with the sponge and dry the surface with a towel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">先用扫帚扫，再用拖把洗，用海绵擦污痕，最后用毛巾擦干表面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you sweep, move the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mop</strong></a>, sponge and towel aside and fetch the broom.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扫地前，把拖把、海绵和毛巾放到一边，拿来扫帚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We used a broom to sweep the room, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mop</strong></a> for the floor, a sponge for the sink and a towel to dry it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们用扫帚扫房间、拖把拖地、海绵洗水槽，最后用毛巾擦干。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a wet mop</span> · 湿拖把</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cleaning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cleaning</a></span> · 清洁</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dustpan&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dustpan</a></span> · 簸箕</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-033">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 扫帚；v. 用扫帚扫</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Sweep with the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=broom&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>broom</strong></a>, wash with the mop, wipe marks with the sponge and dry the surface with a towel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">先用扫帚扫，再用拖把洗，用海绵擦污痕，最后用毛巾擦干表面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you sweep, move the mop, sponge and towel aside and fetch the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=broom&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>broom</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扫地前，把拖把、海绵和毛巾放到一边，拿来扫帚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We used a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=broom&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>broom</strong></a> to sweep the room, a mop for the floor, a sponge for the sink and a towel to dry it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们用扫帚扫房间、拖把拖地、海绵洗水槽，最后用毛巾擦干。</p></li>
</ol>

<summary><h4 id="word-10-046" class="vocabulary-word-title">towel<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-046" aria-label="定位 towel">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-046" data-mark-label="towel" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="towel：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="towel：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=towel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 towel" title="在有道查询 towel">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-046</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 毛巾，手巾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Sweep with the broom, wash with the mop, wipe marks with the sponge and dry the surface with a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=towel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>towel</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">先用扫帚扫，再用拖把洗，用海绵擦污痕，最后用毛巾擦干表面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you sweep, move the mop, sponge and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=towel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>towel</strong></a> aside and fetch the broom.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扫地前，把拖把、海绵和毛巾放到一边，拿来扫帚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We used a broom to sweep the room, a mop for the floor, a sponge for the sink and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=towel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>towel</strong></a> to dry it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们用扫帚扫房间、拖把拖地、海绵洗水槽，最后用毛巾擦干。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a dry towel</span> · 干毛巾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cleaning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cleaning</a></span> · 清洁</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dustpan&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dustpan</a></span> · 簸箕</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-035">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Put the protective pad over the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mattress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mattress</strong></a> and leave the cushion on the chair.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">把保护垫铺在床垫上，把靠垫放在椅子上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mattress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mattress</strong></a> felt firm, but the pad and cushion made the bed more comfortable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">床垫偏硬，但垫子和靠垫让床更舒服。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She bought a new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mattress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mattress</strong></a>, a washable pad and a matching cushion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她买了新床垫、可洗垫子和配套靠垫。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fold the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blanket&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blanket</strong></a> over the quilt and put a clean sheet beneath the pillow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">把毛毯折在被子上，在枕头下面铺干净床单。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The guest bed needs a sheet, a pillow and either a quilt or a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blanket&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blanket</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">客床需要床单、枕头，以及一床被子或毛毯。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She washed the sheet and pillow cover, then aired the quilt and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blanket&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blanket</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她洗了床单和枕套，然后晾晒被子和毛毯。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 10.05｜固定、剃刮、通电与照明 {#group-10-05}

**串联方式：** 按家用任务分四小步：钉牢物件、剃须、连接电线、点火照明；不把这些器具当成同义词。

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=staple&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>staple</strong></a> for the papers and a nail for the wooden frame.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">纸张用订书钉，木框用钉子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A loose <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=staple&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>staple</strong></a> and a bent nail lay on the workbench.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工作台上有一颗松脱的订书钉和一颗弯钉子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The magnet picked up both the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=staple&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>staple</strong></a> and the nail.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">磁铁吸起了订书钉和钉子。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use a clean <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=razor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>razor</strong></a> when you shave.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">剃须时要用干净的剃须刀。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">He prefers to shave with an electric <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=razor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>razor</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他喜欢用电动剃须刀剃须。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A blunt <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=razor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>razor</strong></a> makes it harder to shave smoothly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">钝剃刀难以把胡须刮干净。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A blunt razor makes it harder to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shave</strong></a> smoothly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">钝剃刀难以把胡须刮干净。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">shave carefully</span> · 小心剃须</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-051">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-051" class="vocabulary-word-title">fuse<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-051" aria-label="定位 fuse">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-051" data-mark-label="fuse" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fuse：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fuse：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fuse" title="在有道查询 fuse">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-051</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 保险丝；导火线；v.(使)熔化；(使)融合</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The technician replaced the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fuse</strong></a> and inspected each strand inside the damaged cable and cord.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">技术员更换保险丝，并检查损坏电缆和电线内部的每一股线。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A broken copper strand in the cord caused a fault that blew the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fuse</strong></a> near the cable connection.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">电线中断裂的一股铜丝导致故障，烧断了电缆连接处附近的保险丝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Disconnect the cable and cord before checking the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fuse</strong></a> or touching any exposed strand.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">检查保险丝或触碰任何裸露线股前，先断开电缆和电线。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a blown fuse</span> · 熔断的保险丝</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=insulation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">insulation</a></span> · 绝缘层</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=circuit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">circuit</a></span> · 电路</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-052">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A broken copper strand in the cord caused a fault that blew the fuse near the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cable</strong></a> connection.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">电线中断裂的一股铜丝导致故障，烧断了电缆连接处附近的保险丝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Disconnect the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cable</strong></a> and cord before checking the fuse or touching any exposed strand.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">检查保险丝或触碰任何裸露线股前，先断开电缆和电线。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-055">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 火柴；比赛；匹配的人或物；v. 与……匹配</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Strike a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=match&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>match</strong></a> to light the wax candle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">划根火柴点燃蜡烛。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A drop of wax fell from the candle as she blew out the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=match&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>match</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她吹灭火柴时，一滴蜡从蜡烛上滴下。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Keep the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=match&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>match</strong></a> away from spilled wax when lighting the candle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">点蜡烛时，不要让火柴靠近洒出的蜡。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">strike a match</span> · 划火柴</p>

</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-056">

### 词群 10.06｜整理纸张与办公文具 {#group-10-06}

**串联方式：** 从作品夹、平装书和小册子进入复印、备忘录及文具，再记胶、墨、橡皮与剪刀。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-058">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-058" class="vocabulary-word-title">portfolio<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-058" aria-label="定位 portfolio">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-058" data-mark-label="portfolio" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="portfolio：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="portfolio：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=portfolio&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 portfolio" title="在有道查询 portfolio">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-058</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 文件夹，公事包</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She placed a paperback and a pamphlet inside the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=portfolio&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>portfolio</strong></a>, taking care not to bend the cover.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她把平装书和小册子放进文件夹，小心不折弯封面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=portfolio&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>portfolio</strong></a> contains a pamphlet showing the cover design for the new paperback.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">文件夹里有一本小册子，展示新平装书的封面设计。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A damaged cover reduced the value of the paperback, but the pamphlet in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=portfolio&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>portfolio</strong></a> remained intact.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">封面破损降低了平装书的价值，但文件夹里的小册子完好无损。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an art portfolio</span> · 作品集文件夹</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=binding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">binding</a></span> · 装订</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=booklet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">booklet</a></span> · 小册子</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-061">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-061" class="vocabulary-word-title">tissue<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-061" aria-label="定位 tissue">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-061" data-mark-label="tissue" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tissue：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tissue：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tissue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tissue" title="在有道查询 tissue">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-061</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 薄绢，薄纸；组织；面巾纸</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A Xerox copy of the memorandum and its duplicate were wrapped in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tissue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tissue</strong></a> paper.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">备忘录的一份施乐复印件及其副本用薄纸包着。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Remove the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tissue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tissue</strong></a> paper before making a Xerox duplicate of the memorandum.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">给备忘录制作施乐复印副本前，先取下薄包装纸。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The archive kept the original memorandum under <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tissue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tissue</strong></a> paper and supplied a Xerox duplicate for readers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">档案馆把备忘录原件覆在薄纸下，向读者提供施乐复印副本。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">tissue paper</span> · 薄包装纸</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=photocopy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">photocopy</a></span> · 复印件</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=archive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">archive</a></span> · 档案</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<summary><h4 id="word-10-065" class="vocabulary-word-title">memorandum<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-065" aria-label="定位 memorandum">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-065" data-mark-label="memorandum" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="memorandum：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="memorandum：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=memorandum&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 memorandum" title="在有道查询 memorandum">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-065</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 备忘录；建议书；(= memo)(公司或组织内部的)公务便条</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A Xerox copy of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=memorandum&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>memorandum</strong></a> and its duplicate were wrapped in tissue paper.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">备忘录的一份施乐复印件及其副本用薄纸包着。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Remove the tissue paper before making a Xerox duplicate of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=memorandum&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>memorandum</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">给备忘录制作施乐复印副本前，先取下薄包装纸。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The archive kept the original <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=memorandum&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>memorandum</strong></a> under tissue paper and supplied a Xerox duplicate for readers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">档案馆把备忘录原件覆在薄纸下，向读者提供施乐复印副本。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an internal memorandum</span> · 内部备忘录</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=photocopy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">photocopy</a></span> · 复印件</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=archive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">archive</a></span> · 档案</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-066">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stationery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stationery</strong></a> drawer holds glue, ink, a rubber and scissors.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">文具抽屉里有胶水、墨水、一块橡皮和剪刀。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Buy scissors and a rubber at the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stationery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stationery</strong></a> shop, but check the glue and ink first.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">去文具店买剪刀和橡皮，但先检查一下胶水和墨水。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The art class used <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stationery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stationery</strong></a> including scissors, coloured ink, glue and a rubber for pencil corrections.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">美术课用了剪刀、彩色墨水、胶水，以及修改铅笔画的橡皮等文具。</p></li>
</ol>
