### 词群 10.07｜看清物品的局部结构 {#group-10-07}

**串联方式：** 由剪切和边缘进入组成部分、标签、徽章，再辨门窗的插销、把手、百叶窗和玻璃。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-071">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-071" class="vocabulary-word-title">shear<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-071" aria-label="定位 shear">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-071" data-mark-label="shear" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="shear：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="shear：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 shear" title="在有道查询 shear">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-071</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. [~s](尤指修剪篱笆用的)大剪刀；v. 剪</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The blade’s sharp edge can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shear</strong></a> material laid across the rim of the bowl.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">刀刃能剪切横放在碗沿的材料。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Keep the cutting edge clear of the metal rim when you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shear</strong></a> the sheet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">剪薄片时，别让刃口碰到金属边框。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The machine can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shear</strong></a> the rim cleanly, leaving a smooth edge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机器能利落剪切边框，留下平滑边缘。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">shear wool</span> · 剪羊毛</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">blade</a></span> · 刀刃</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cutting%20tool&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cutting tool</a></span> · 切割工具</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-072">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-072" class="vocabulary-word-title">edge<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-072" aria-label="定位 edge">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-072" data-mark-label="edge" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="edge：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="edge：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=edge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 edge" title="在有道查询 edge">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-072</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 边缘，边；刀口</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The blade’s sharp <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=edge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>edge</strong></a> can shear material laid across the rim of the bowl.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">刀刃能剪切横放在碗沿的材料。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Keep the cutting <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=edge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>edge</strong></a> clear of the metal rim when you shear the sheet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">剪薄片时，别让刃口碰到金属边框。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The machine can shear the rim cleanly, leaving a smooth <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=edge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>edge</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机器能利落剪切边框，留下平滑边缘。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a sharp edge</span> · 锋利边缘</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">blade</a></span> · 刀刃</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cutting%20tool&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cutting tool</a></span> · 切割工具</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-073">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Keep the cutting edge clear of the metal <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rim</strong></a> when you shear the sheet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">剪薄片时，别让刃口碰到金属边框。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The machine can shear the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rim</strong></a> cleanly, leaving a smooth edge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机器能利落剪切边框，留下平滑边缘。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-074">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-074" class="vocabulary-word-title">element<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-074" aria-label="定位 element">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-074" data-mark-label="element" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="element：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="element：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=element&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 element" title="在有道查询 element">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-074</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.(化学中的)元素；组成部分；基本部分</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This section examines how each structural <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=element&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>element</strong></a> affects safety, a key factor in the design.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这一节考察每个结构构件如何影响安全，而安全是设计的关键因素。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The damaged <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=element&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>element</strong></a> in the roof section became a major factor in the repair cost.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">屋顶部分损坏的构件成为维修费用的重要影响因素。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report identifies corrosion as a risk factor for every metal <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=element&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>element</strong></a> in this section of the bridge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告指出，腐蚀是桥梁这一部分每个金属构件的风险因素。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a structural element</span> · 结构构件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=component&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">component</a></span> · 部件</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=structure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">structure</a></span> · 结构</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-075">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This section examines how each structural element affects safety, a key <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=factor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>factor</strong></a> in the design.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这一节考察每个结构构件如何影响安全，而安全是设计的关键因素。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The damaged element in the roof section became a major <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=factor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>factor</strong></a> in the repair cost.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">屋顶部分损坏的构件成为维修费用的重要影响因素。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report identifies corrosion as a risk <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=factor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>factor</strong></a> for every metal element in this section of the bridge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告指出，腐蚀是桥梁这一部分每个金属构件的风险因素。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The damaged element in the roof <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=section&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>section</strong></a> became a major factor in the repair cost.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">屋顶部分损坏的构件成为维修费用的重要影响因素。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report identifies corrosion as a risk factor for every metal element in this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=section&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>section</strong></a> of the bridge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告指出，腐蚀是桥梁这一部分每个金属构件的风险因素。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The jacket has a price <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tag&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tag</strong></a> and a care label, while the employee wears an identity badge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">夹克上有价签和护理标签，员工佩戴着身份胸牌。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Remove the tag, read the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=label&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>label</strong></a> and attach your badge before wearing the uniform.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">穿制服前，取下吊牌、阅读标签并别上胸牌。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The jacket has a price tag and a care label, while the employee wears an identity <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=badge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>badge</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">夹克上有价签和护理标签，员工佩戴着身份胸牌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Remove the tag, read the label and attach your <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=badge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>badge</strong></a> before wearing the uniform.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">穿制服前，取下吊牌、阅读标签并别上胸牌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The security <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=badge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>badge</strong></a> was clipped beside the name label on a bag with a luggage tag.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">安全证件夹在包的姓名标签旁，包上还有行李吊牌。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an identity badge</span> · 身份胸牌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=identification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">identification</a></span> · 身份识别</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">marking</a></span> · 标记</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-080">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-080" class="vocabulary-word-title">bolt<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-080" aria-label="定位 bolt">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-080" data-mark-label="bolt" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bolt：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bolt：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bolt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bolt" title="在有道查询 bolt">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-080</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 螺栓；插销；v. 闩上(门、窗)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Turn the knob, release the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bolt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bolt</strong></a> and pull the handle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">转动旋钮、打开门闩，再拉把手。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The carpenter replaced the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bolt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bolt</strong></a> and fitted a new knob and handle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">木匠更换了门闩，并装上新旋钮和把手。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A loose <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bolt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bolt</strong></a> allowed the handle to move even when the knob was locked.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一颗松动的螺栓使把手在旋钮锁住时仍能活动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a door bolt</span> · 门闩</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=latch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">latch</a></span> · 门扣</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hinge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hinge</a></span> · 铰链</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-081">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-081" class="vocabulary-word-title">knob<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-081" aria-label="定位 knob">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-081" data-mark-label="knob" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="knob：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="knob：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=knob&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 knob" title="在有道查询 knob">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-081</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 球形把手；旋钮</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Turn the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=knob&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>knob</strong></a>, release the bolt and pull the handle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">转动旋钮、打开门闩，再拉把手。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The carpenter replaced the bolt and fitted a new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=knob&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>knob</strong></a> and handle.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">木匠更换了门闩，并装上新旋钮和把手。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A loose bolt allowed the handle to move even when the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=knob&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>knob</strong></a> was locked.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一颗松动的螺栓使把手在旋钮锁住时仍能活动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a door knob</span> · 球形门把手</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=latch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">latch</a></span> · 门扣</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hinge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hinge</a></span> · 铰链</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-082">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 柄；把手；v. 拿；处理</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The carpenter replaced the bolt and fitted a new knob and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=handle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>handle</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">木匠更换了门闩，并装上新旋钮和把手。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A loose bolt allowed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=handle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>handle</strong></a> to move even when the knob was locked.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一颗松动的螺栓使把手在旋钮锁住时仍能活动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a door handle</span> · 门把手</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=latch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">latch</a></span> · 门扣</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hinge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hinge</a></span> · 铰链</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-083">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-083" class="vocabulary-word-title">shutter<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-083" aria-label="定位 shutter">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-083" data-mark-label="shutter" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="shutter：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="shutter：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shutter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 shutter" title="在有道查询 shutter">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-083</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 百叶窗；(相机)快门；v. 停止(营业)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shutter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shutter</strong></a> and curtain increase privacy when the window pane has little opacity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">窗玻璃透明度高时，百叶窗和窗帘能增强私密性。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We compared the opacity of the curtain with that of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shutter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shutter</strong></a> and the frosted pane.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们比较了窗帘、百叶窗和磨砂玻璃的不透明程度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The designer chose a low-opacity pane, a light curtain and an adjustable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shutter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shutter</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">设计师选择了低不透明度的玻璃、轻薄窗帘和可调百叶窗。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a wooden shutter</span> · 木制百叶窗</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=translucent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">translucent</a></span> · 半透明的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=privacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">privacy</a></span> · 隐私</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-084">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-084" class="vocabulary-word-title">curtain<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-084" aria-label="定位 curtain">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-084" data-mark-label="curtain" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="curtain：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="curtain：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=curtain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 curtain" title="在有道查询 curtain">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-084</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 窗帘；帘；幕布；v. 给……装上帘子</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shutter and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=curtain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>curtain</strong></a> increase privacy when the window pane has little opacity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">窗玻璃透明度高时，百叶窗和窗帘能增强私密性。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We compared the opacity of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=curtain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>curtain</strong></a> with that of the shutter and the frosted pane.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们比较了窗帘、百叶窗和磨砂玻璃的不透明程度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The designer chose a low-opacity pane, a light <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=curtain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>curtain</strong></a> and an adjustable shutter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">设计师选择了低不透明度的玻璃、轻薄窗帘和可调百叶窗。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a heavy curtain</span> · 厚窗帘</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=translucent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">translucent</a></span> · 半透明的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=privacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">privacy</a></span> · 隐私</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-085">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-085" class="vocabulary-word-title">pane<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-085" aria-label="定位 pane">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-085" data-mark-label="pane" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pane：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pane：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pane&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pane" title="在有道查询 pane">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-085</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.(一块)窗玻璃</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shutter and curtain increase privacy when the window <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pane&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pane</strong></a> has little opacity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">窗玻璃透明度高时，百叶窗和窗帘能增强私密性。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We compared the opacity of the curtain with that of the shutter and the frosted <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pane&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pane</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们比较了窗帘、百叶窗和磨砂玻璃的不透明程度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The designer chose a low-opacity <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pane&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pane</strong></a>, a light curtain and an adjustable shutter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">设计师选择了低不透明度的玻璃、轻薄窗帘和可调百叶窗。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a window pane</span> · 一块窗玻璃</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=translucent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">translucent</a></span> · 半透明的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=privacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">privacy</a></span> · 隐私</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-086">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-086" class="vocabulary-word-title">opacity<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-086" aria-label="定位 opacity">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-086" data-mark-label="opacity" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="opacity：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="opacity：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=opacity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 opacity" title="在有道查询 opacity">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-086</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.不透明性</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shutter and curtain increase privacy when the window pane has little <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=opacity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>opacity</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">窗玻璃透明度高时，百叶窗和窗帘能增强私密性。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We compared the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=opacity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>opacity</strong></a> of the curtain with that of the shutter and the frosted pane.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们比较了窗帘、百叶窗和磨砂玻璃的不透明程度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The designer chose a low-<a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=opacity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>opacity</strong></a> pane, a light curtain and an adjustable shutter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">设计师选择了低不透明度的玻璃、轻薄窗帘和可调百叶窗。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">glass opacity</span> · 玻璃不透明度</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=translucent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">translucent</a></span> · 半透明的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=privacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">privacy</a></span> · 隐私</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 10.08｜用不同容器装东西 {#group-10-08}

**串联方式：** 对比罐、桶、提桶及金属罐，想象每种容器的形状和用途。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-087">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-087" class="vocabulary-word-title">jar<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-087" aria-label="定位 jar">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-087" data-mark-label="jar" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="jar：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="jar：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 jar" title="在有道查询 jar">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-087</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 罐，广口瓶；猛烈震动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fill the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jar</strong></a> from the barrel, then rinse the bucket, pail and empty can.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">从大木桶给玻璃罐灌满，再冲洗水桶、提桶和空铁罐。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The storage shed contained a barrel, a bucket, a pail and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jar</strong></a> beside an old metal can.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">储藏棚里有大桶、水桶、提桶和广口瓶，旁边还有旧金属罐。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use the bucket or pail to carry water from the barrel, then pour it into a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jar</strong></a> or can.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用水桶或提桶从大桶运水，再倒进广口瓶或铁罐。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a glass jar</span> · 玻璃罐</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=container&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">container</a></span> · 容器</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=capacity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">capacity</a></span> · 容量</li></ul>
</div>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The storage shed contained a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barrel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>barrel</strong></a>, a bucket, a pail and a jar beside an old metal can.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">储藏棚里有大桶、水桶、提桶和广口瓶，旁边还有旧金属罐。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The storage shed contained a barrel, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bucket&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bucket</strong></a>, a pail and a jar beside an old metal can.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">储藏棚里有大桶、水桶、提桶和广口瓶，旁边还有旧金属罐。</p></li>

### 词群 10.09｜选择家电与照明设备 {#group-10-09}

**串联方式：** 按通信、摄影、照明、制冷、清洁和通风任务认识设备，并找到开关。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-091">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-091" class="vocabulary-word-title">phone<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-091" aria-label="定位 phone">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-091" data-mark-label="phone" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="phone：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="phone：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=phone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 phone" title="在有道查询 phone">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-091</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 电话；电话机；电话昕筒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The portable camera sends an image to your <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=phone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>phone</strong></a> when someone rings the bell.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有人按铃时，便携摄像机会把图像发送到手机。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">His portable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=phone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>phone</strong></a> had a camera and a bell-like ringtone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他的便携电话有摄像头，铃声像钟声。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A portable camera beside the bell lets residents check visitors on a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=phone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>phone</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">门铃旁的便携摄像头让居民能用手机查看访客。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a mobile phone</span> · 手机</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=doorbell&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">doorbell</a></span> · 门铃</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=notification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">notification</a></span> · 通知</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-092">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-092" class="vocabulary-word-title">bell<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-092" aria-label="定位 bell">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-092" data-mark-label="bell" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bell：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bell：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bell&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bell" title="在有道查询 bell">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-092</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 钟；铃</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The portable camera sends an image to your phone when someone rings the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bell&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bell</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有人按铃时，便携摄像机会把图像发送到手机。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">His portable phone had a camera and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bell&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bell</strong></a>-like ringtone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他的便携电话有摄像头，铃声像钟声。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A portable camera beside the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bell&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bell</strong></a> lets residents check visitors on a phone.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">门铃旁的便携摄像头让居民能用手机查看访客。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-095">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-095" class="vocabulary-word-title">spotlight<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-095" aria-label="定位 spotlight">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-095" data-mark-label="spotlight" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="spotlight：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="spotlight：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spotlight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 spotlight" title="在有道查询 spotlight">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-095</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 聚光灯；聚光灯照出的光圈</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spotlight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spotlight</strong></a> needs a new bulb, so use the lantern or flashlight for now.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">聚光灯需要换灯泡，暂时先用灯笼或手电筒。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A single bulb lit the lantern, while a flashlight beam crossed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spotlight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spotlight</strong></a> on stage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一个灯泡点亮了灯笼，手电筒光束穿过舞台聚光灯的光圈。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The technician checked the bulb in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spotlight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spotlight</strong></a> before packing the lantern and flashlight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">技术员先检查聚光灯的灯泡，再打包灯笼和手电筒。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a stage spotlight</span> · 舞台聚光灯</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=illumination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">illumination</a></span> · 照明</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=beam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">beam</a></span> · 光束</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A single bulb lit the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lantern&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lantern</strong></a>, while a flashlight beam crossed the spotlight on stage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一个灯泡点亮了灯笼，手电筒光束穿过舞台聚光灯的光圈。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The technician checked the bulb in the spotlight before packing the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lantern&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lantern</strong></a> and flashlight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">技术员先检查聚光灯的灯泡，再打包灯笼和手电筒。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A single <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bulb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bulb</strong></a> lit the lantern, while a flashlight beam crossed the spotlight on stage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一个灯泡点亮了灯笼，手电筒光束穿过舞台聚光灯的光圈。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-099">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-099" class="vocabulary-word-title">refrigerator<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-099" aria-label="定位 refrigerator">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-099" data-mark-label="refrigerator" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="refrigerator：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="refrigerator：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refrigerator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 refrigerator" title="在有道查询 refrigerator">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-099</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 冰箱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refrigerator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refrigerator</strong></a> manual calls the appliance a fridge in its quick-start guide.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">冰箱说明书在快速入门指南中把该电器简称为 fridge。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Our old <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refrigerator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refrigerator</strong></a> was noisy, but the new fridge is quiet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们的旧冰箱很吵，新冰箱则很安静。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A large <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refrigerator&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refrigerator</strong></a> uses more space than the compact fridge in the office.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大冰箱比办公室的小冰箱占用更多空间。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Turn off the fan at the switch before using the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vacuum&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vacuum</strong></a> cleaner nearby.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在附近使用吸尘器前，先用开关关掉风扇。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vacuum&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vacuum</strong></a> cleaner and fan share a socket with a separate safety switch.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">吸尘器和风扇共用一个带独立安全开关的插座。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She pressed the switch on the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vacuum&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vacuum</strong></a> cleaner while the fan dried the floor.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">风扇吹干地板时，她按下了吸尘器开关。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 10.10｜家具、辅助器具与出行用品 {#group-10-10}

**串联方式：** 从围栏和踏板到架子、梯子、升降设备、凳子和抽屉，再拿起雨具出门；dredge 是工程延伸。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-104">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-104" class="vocabulary-word-title">hurdle<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-104" aria-label="定位 hurdle">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-104" data-mark-label="hurdle" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hurdle：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hurdle：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurdle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hurdle" title="在有道查询 hurdle">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-104</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 栏架；跨栏赛跑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rider stopped pressing the pedal and lifted the bicycle over a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurdle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hurdle</strong></a> beside the fence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">骑手不再踩踏板，把自行车抬过围栏旁的障碍栏架。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A loose pedal made it difficult to ride past the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurdle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hurdle</strong></a> near the fence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">松动的踏板使人难以骑过围栏附近的障碍栏架。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The training course has a low <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurdle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hurdle</strong></a>, a wooden fence and a station for practising pedal control.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">训练路线设有矮栏架、木围栏和练习踏板控制的区域。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">clear a hurdle</span> · 越过栏架</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obstacle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">obstacle</a></span> · 障碍物</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cycling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cycling</a></span> · 骑行</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-105">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-105" class="vocabulary-word-title">fence<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-105" aria-label="定位 fence">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-105" data-mark-label="fence" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fence：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fence：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fence" title="在有道查询 fence">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-105</span></span></summary>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rider stopped pressing the pedal and lifted the bicycle over a hurdle beside the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fence</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">骑手不再踩踏板，把自行车抬过围栏旁的障碍栏架。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A loose pedal made it difficult to ride past the hurdle near the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fence</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">松动的踏板使人难以骑过围栏附近的障碍栏架。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The training course has a low hurdle, a wooden <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fence</strong></a> and a station for practising pedal control.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">训练路线设有矮栏架、木围栏和练习踏板控制的区域。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rider stopped pressing the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pedal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pedal</strong></a> and lifted the bicycle over a hurdle beside the fence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">骑手不再踩踏板，把自行车抬过围栏旁的障碍栏架。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A loose <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pedal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pedal</strong></a> made it difficult to ride past the hurdle near the fence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">松动的踏板使人难以骑过围栏附近的障碍栏架。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The training course has a low hurdle, a wooden fence and a station for practising <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pedal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pedal</strong></a> control.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">训练路线设有矮栏架、木围栏和练习踏板控制的区域。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a bicycle pedal</span> · 自行车踏板</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obstacle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">obstacle</a></span> · 障碍物</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cycling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cycling</a></span> · 骑行</li></ul>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Use the ladder rather than the stool to lift the drawer onto the high <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shelf&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shelf</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">用梯子而不是凳子，把抽屉抬到高架子上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A ladder stood beside the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shelf&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shelf</strong></a>, with a stool below the drawer we needed to lift.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">梯子立在架子旁，凳子位于我们需要抬起的抽屉下方。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We had to lift the stool out of the way before moving the ladder and removing the drawer from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shelf&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shelf</strong></a> unit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">移动梯子并从架柜拆下抽屉前，我们必须先抬走凳子。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A ladder stood beside the shelf, with a stool below the drawer we needed to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lift&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lift</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">梯子立在架子旁，凳子位于我们需要抬起的抽屉下方。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-112">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 伞，雨伞</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Take an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=umbrella&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>umbrella</strong></a> and a raincoat in case the storm gets worse.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">带上雨伞和雨衣，以防暴雨加剧。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her raincoat kept her dry when the wind broke the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=umbrella&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>umbrella</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">风吹坏雨伞时，雨衣使她保持干爽。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shop sells a raincoat that folds into a bag beside a compact <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=umbrella&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>umbrella</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">店里售卖可折叠收进袋子的雨衣，旁边是小巧雨伞。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-113">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 雨衣</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Take an umbrella and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=raincoat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>raincoat</strong></a> in case the storm gets worse.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">带上雨伞和雨衣，以防暴雨加剧。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=raincoat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>raincoat</strong></a> kept her dry when the wind broke the umbrella.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">风吹坏雨伞时，雨衣使她保持干爽。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shop sells a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=raincoat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>raincoat</strong></a> that folds into a bag beside a compact umbrella.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">店里售卖可折叠收进袋子的雨衣，旁边是小巧雨伞。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-114">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-114" class="vocabulary-word-title">dredge<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-114" aria-label="定位 dredge">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-114" data-mark-label="dredge" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dredge：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dredge：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dredge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dredge" title="在有道查询 dredge">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-114</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 挖泥船，挖掘机；v. 挖掘；撒(糖、面粉等)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council plans to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dredge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dredge</strong></a> the harbour to remove accumulated mud.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政计划疏浚港口，清除积泥。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A specialised vessel can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dredge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dredge</strong></a> the channel without closing the entire port.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">专业船只可以在不关闭整个港口的情况下疏浚航道。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dredge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dredge</strong></a> the riverbed before larger ships arrive.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大型船只到达前，工人将疏浚河床。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">dredge a harbour</span> · 疏浚港口</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=silt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">silt</a></span> · 淤泥</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dredging&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dredging</a></span> · 疏浚作业</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 10.11｜从开采到金属与建材 {#group-10-11}

**串联方式：** 先采矿、加工与锻造，再辨金属、合金和建材，最后联系燃料和润滑。

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A metal workshop beside the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mill</strong></a> can forge an iron part or machine an alloy component.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工厂旁的金属车间能锻造铁件或加工合金部件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mill</strong></a> recycles scrap metal, and specialists forge the resulting iron alloy into durable fittings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工厂回收废金属，专业人员把所得铁合金锻造成耐用配件。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The restoration team tested the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lead&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lead</strong></a> pipes, brass handles and bronze ornaments.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修复团队检测了铅管、黄铜把手和青铜饰品。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The builders used <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cement</strong></a> for the foundation, pitch for waterproofing and lime plaster for the walls.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">建筑工人用水泥打地基、沥青防水，并用石灰灰泥抹墙。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey found <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cement</strong></a> repairs, a pitch coating and traces of old lime plaster.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">勘察发现水泥修补处、沥青涂层和旧石灰灰泥痕迹。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Store the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cement</strong></a> and lime away from the pitch before mixing the plaster.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调制灰泥前，把水泥和石灰存放在远离沥青的地方。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey found cement repairs, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pitch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pitch</strong></a> coating and traces of old lime plaster.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">勘察发现水泥修补处、沥青涂层和旧石灰灰泥痕迹。</p></li>

</ol>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 矿，矿井；地雷</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers at the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mine</strong></a> refuel equipment near the pit and lubricate it before moving the fuel containers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">矿工在矿坑附近给设备加油，并在移动燃料容器前给设备润滑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The open-pit <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mine</strong></a> needs fuel for its trucks and oil to lubricate their engines.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">露天矿需要给卡车供燃料，还需要油来润滑发动机。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before entering the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mine</strong></a> pit, check the fuel level and lubricate the moving parts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">进入矿坑前，检查燃料量并润滑活动部件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a coal mine</span> · 煤矿</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-141">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-141" class="vocabulary-word-title">pit<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-141" aria-label="定位 pit">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-141" data-mark-label="pit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pit：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pit：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pit" title="在有道查询 pit">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-141</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers at the mine refuel equipment near the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pit</strong></a> and lubricate it before moving the fuel containers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">矿工在矿坑附近给设备加油，并在移动燃料容器前给设备润滑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The open-<a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pit</strong></a> mine needs fuel for its trucks and oil to lubricate their engines.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">露天矿需要给卡车供燃料，还需要油来润滑发动机。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before entering the mine <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pit</strong></a>, check the fuel level and lubricate the moving parts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">进入矿坑前，检查燃料量并润滑活动部件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an open pit</span> · 露天矿坑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=extraction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">extraction</a></span> · 开采</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=machinery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">machinery</a></span> · 机械设备</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The open-pit mine needs <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fuel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fuel</strong></a> for its trucks and oil to lubricate their engines.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">露天矿需要给卡车供燃料，还需要油来润滑发动机。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before entering the mine pit, check the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fuel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fuel</strong></a> level and lubricate the moving parts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">进入矿坑前，检查燃料量并润滑活动部件。</p></li>

### 词群 10.12｜软材料、纺织与木材 {#group-10-12}

**串联方式：** 从皮革、塑料和纤维进入编织与织物，再对照木材及木制品。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-128">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-128" class="vocabulary-word-title">leather<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-128" aria-label="定位 leather">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-128" data-mark-label="leather" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="leather：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="leather：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 leather" title="在有道查询 leather">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-128</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 皮革；(尤指骑摩托车者穿的)皮衣</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The bag combines <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>leather</strong></a> with fabric made from recycled plastic fibre.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这个包把皮革与再生塑料纤维制成的布料结合起来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Manufacturers compare <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>leather</strong></a>, plastic and natural fibre when choosing fabric for seats.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">厂家选择座椅面料时，会比较皮革、塑料和天然纤维。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The fabric feels like <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>leather</strong></a> but contains a synthetic fibre derived from plastic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种布料摸起来像皮革，但含有源自塑料的合成纤维。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">genuine leather</span> · 真皮</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=synthetic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">synthetic</a></span> · 合成的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sustainability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sustainability</a></span> · 可持续性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-129">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-129" class="vocabulary-word-title">plastic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-129" aria-label="定位 plastic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-129" data-mark-label="plastic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="plastic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="plastic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plastic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 plastic" title="在有道查询 plastic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-129</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 塑料；adj. 有塑性的；塑料的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The bag combines leather with fabric made from recycled <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plastic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plastic</strong></a> fibre.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这个包把皮革与再生塑料纤维制成的布料结合起来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Manufacturers compare leather, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plastic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plastic</strong></a> and natural fibre when choosing fabric for seats.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">厂家选择座椅面料时，会比较皮革、塑料和天然纤维。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The fabric feels like leather but contains a synthetic fibre derived from <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plastic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plastic</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种布料摸起来像皮革，但含有源自塑料的合成纤维。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">recycled plastic</span> · 再生塑料</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=synthetic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">synthetic</a></span> · 合成的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sustainability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sustainability</a></span> · 可持续性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 尼龙</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers tied the lumber with <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nylon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nylon</strong></a> rope before loading the wooden cart.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人用尼龙绳捆好木材，再装上木车。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The wooden rack holds lumber, with spare <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nylon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nylon</strong></a> straps hanging beside it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">木架上放着木材，旁边挂着备用尼龙绑带。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shelter uses lumber for its wooden supports and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nylon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nylon</strong></a> sheets for its roof.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">棚屋用木材做木支架，用尼龙布做屋顶。</p></li>
</ol>

</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers tied the lumber with nylon rope before loading the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wooden&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wooden</strong></a> cart.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人用尼龙绳捆好木材，再装上木车。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wooden&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wooden</strong></a> rack holds lumber, with spare nylon straps hanging beside it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">木架上放着木材，旁边挂着备用尼龙绑带。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shelter uses lumber for its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wooden&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wooden</strong></a> supports and nylon sheets for its roof.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">棚屋用木材做木支架，用尼龙布做屋顶。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a wooden frame</span> · 木框</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=timber&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">timber</a></span> · 木材</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=joinery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">joinery</a></span> · 细木工</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 10.13｜评价材料与商品品质 {#group-10-13}

**串联方式：** 先辨钻石、水晶，再评价真伪、等级、尺寸、易碎性、耐用性和可获得性。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-144">
<summary><h4 id="word-10-144" class="vocabulary-word-title">diamond<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-10-144" aria-label="定位 diamond">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="10-144" data-mark-label="diamond" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="diamond：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="diamond：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diamond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 diamond" title="在有道查询 diamond">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">10-144</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 金刚石，钻石；菱形</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The counterfeit ring contained an inferior crystal instead of a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diamond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>diamond</strong></a>, making it a fake.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">假冒戒指用劣质水晶代替钻石，因此是假货。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An expert identified the fake <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diamond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>diamond</strong></a> as inferior crystal in a counterfeit luxury setting.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">专家认出，仿冒奢侈品牌戒托中的假钻石其实是劣质水晶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shop sold a crystal ornament honestly, but labelled an inferior fake as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diamond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>diamond</strong></a> in its counterfeit collection.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这家店如实出售水晶饰品，却把仿冒系列中的一件劣质假货标为钻石。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a diamond ring</span> · 钻戒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=authenticity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">authenticity</a></span> · 真实性</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gemstone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">gemstone</a></span> · 宝石</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="10-149">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=miniature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>miniature</strong></a> model is available in fragile glass or durable metal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这个微型模型有易碎玻璃版和耐用金属版可选。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A durable case is available to protect the fragile <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=miniature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>miniature</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有耐用保护盒可以买来保护这件易碎微型模型。</p></li>

</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.易碎的；脆弱的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **raw materials**：原材料。
- **durable household items**：耐用的家居用品；household 在第 19 章出现。
- **recycle and reuse materials**：回收并重复利用材料。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

Before buying a household item, we can consider its material, quality and purpose. A durable product may last longer than a fragile one. Some objects can be repaired, and some materials can be recycled or reused. This helps us connect everyday purchases with the amount of waste we produce.

**中文理解：** 购买家居用品前，可以考虑材料、质量和用途。耐用的产品可能比易碎的产品使用更久。有些物品可以维修，有些材料可以回收再用。这样就能把日常消费与产生的垃圾量联系起来。

### 闭卷回忆｜先尝试，再逐级提示

**任务：** 选一件家居用品，介绍它的材料、部件、用途、质量及报废去向。

1. 关掉本章正文，在空白页列出你记得的词群；每群先写 3—5 个英文词或搭配。
2. 从中选 3 个词群，连成 3—5 句英文；用明确关系连接，专业分类和事实不确定时先核对。
3. 卡住时，先只看“本章主线”；还想不起，再看词群标题和中文简释，最后才看英文。
4. 用核对编号找出遗漏词；把“见到认得”与“想表达时能说出”分开记录。
5. 合上提示重新说一遍；下次换一个对象、地点或例子，检查能否迁移。
